5. DATA LIMITATIONS
==============================================================================
NPC counts represent entries in configured squad compositions.
They do not prove that all listed NPCs are simultaneously spawned.
Coordinates are patrol path points extracted from all.spawn.
They do not prove current live NPC positions.
Conditions and targets are preserved as source configuration text.
